theorem pi_div_two_add_two_sin_cos_gt_sum_sin_two (a b c : ℝ) (ha : 0 < a) (hb : 0 < b) (hc : 0 < c) (ha' : a < Real.pi / 2) (hb' : b < Real.pi / 2) (hc' : c < Real.pi / 2) : Real.pi / 2 + 2 * Real.sin a * Real.cos b + 2 * Real.sin b * Real.cos c > Real.sin (2 * a) + Real.sin (2 * b) + Real.sin (2 * c) := by sorry
theorem sum_sin_le_sum_cos (θ : Fin 10 → ℝ) (h₀ : ∀ i, 0 ≤ θ i) (h₁ : ∀ i, θ i ≤ Real.pi / 2) (h₂ : ∑ i, (Real.sin (θ i))^2 = 1) : 3 * ∑ i, Real.sin (θ i) ≤ ∑ i, Real.cos (θ i) := by sorry
theorem neg_four_mul_prod_nonpos_iff (x : ℝ) : -4 * (x - 5) * (x + 4) * (x - 1) ≤ 0 ↔ x ∈ Set.Icc (-4 : ℝ) 1 ∪ Set.Ici (5 : ℝ) := by sorry
theorem sum_triple_products_le (a b c d : ℝ) (ha : 0 ≤ a) (hb : 0 ≤ b) (hc : 0 ≤ c) (hd : 0 ≤ d) (hsum : a + b + c + d = 1) : a * b * c + a * b * d + a * c * d + b * c * d ≤ 1 / 27 + 176 / 27 * (a * b * c * d) := by sorry
theorem count_ordered_pairs : ((Finset.Icc (-20 : ℤ) 20 ×ˢ Finset.Icc (-20 : ℤ) 20).filter (fun p : ℤ × ℤ => p.1 ^ 2 + p.2 ^ 2 < 16 ∧ p.1 ^ 2 + p.2 ^ 2 < 8 * p.1 ∧ p.1 ^ 2 + p.2 ^ 2 < 8 * p.2)).card = 6 := by sorry
theorem unique_root_iff (θ : ℝ) : (∃! x : ℝ, Real.sin θ * x ^ 2 + (Real.cos θ + Real.tan θ) * x + 1 = 0) ↔ (Real.sin θ = 0 ∨ Real.sin θ = (Real.sqrt 5 - 1) / 2) := by sorry
theorem quadruple_unique (a b c d : ℕ) (ha : 0 < a) (hb : 0 < b) (hc : 0 < c) (hd : 0 < d) : 20 ^ a + 14 ^ (2 * b) = (a + 2 * b + c) ^ (c * d) ↔ (a = 1 ∧ b = 1 ∧ c = 3 ∧ d = 1) := by sorry
theorem exists_prefix_sum_ge_poly_on_unit_interval (a : List ℝ) (ha : a ≠ []) : ∃ k : ℕ, 1 ≤ k ∧ k ≤ a.length ∧ ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc (0 : ℝ) 1, (∑ i ∈ Finset.range a.length, a.getD i 0 * x ^ i) ≤ (a.take k).sum := by sorry
theorem largest_prime_below_100_dividing_central_binom : IsGreatest {p : ℕ | p.Prime ∧ p < 100 ∧ p ∣ Nat.choose 200 100} 61 := by sorry
theorem min_value_sqrt_sum : IsLeast {y : ℝ | ∃ x : ℝ, x ∈ Set.Icc (-1 : ℝ) 1 ∧ y = Real.sqrt (58 - 42 * x) + Real.sqrt (149 - 140 * Real.sqrt (1 - x ^ 2))} (Real.sqrt 109) := by sorry
